TL;DR: 本文研究了非奇异黑洞-白洞跃迁过程中零测地线的散焦效应，指出该过程必然违反零收敛条件，且这一结论不依赖于具体的引力场方程或奇点分辨机制；作者通过广义Painlevé-Gullstrand坐标下的显式反弹几何模型（如Bardeen型质量函数）验证了该结论，并指出瞬时跃迁将导致零收敛条件的无界违反，暗示连续时空度规失效，需量子引力描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非奇异黑洞-白洞跃迁的动力学机制及其对经典能量条件（特别是零收敛条件）的影响，以厘清奇点分辨是否可兼容经典几何框架。

Method: 基于时空运动学分析零标量膨胀θ₊和θ₋在捕获与反捕获视界上的行为，结合Raychaudhuri方程推导必要条件；构造广义Painlevé-Gullstrand坐标下含时正则黑洞解（如Bardeen型），实现黑洞→白洞的平滑过渡几何。

Result: 证明任何动力学非奇异黑洞-白洞跃迁必须局部违反零收敛条件（即Rₐᵦkᵃkᵇ < 0），且该违反严格局域于捕获区收缩与反捕获区形成之间的中间相；瞬时跃迁对应零收敛条件的无界违反。

Conclusion: 非奇异黑洞-白洞跃迁本质上是非经典的——它不可在满足平均零能量条件（ANEC）或零收敛条件的爱因斯坦框架内实现；其物理实现必然依赖于强量子引力效应或有效负能密度源。

Abstract: We investigate the defocusing of null geodesics in dynamical, non-singular black-hole-to-white-hole transitions. Working at the level of spacetime kinematics, and without assuming any specific gravitational field equations, we show that the contraction and disappearance of a trapped region, as well as the subsequent formation and expansion of an anti-trapped region, necessarily require a violation of the null convergence condition. This conclusion follows directly from the behaviour of the null expansions across the trapping and anti-trapping horizons, and is therefore independent of the microscopic mechanism responsible for singularity resolution. We then illustrate this general argument by constructing a class of explicit bouncing geometries in generalised Painlevé-Gullstrand coordinates, obtained by promoting static regular black holes with de Sitter cores to time-dependent black-hole-to-white-hole transition models. For a Bardeen-type mass function, we show that the required violation of the null convergence condition is localised within the intermediate dynamical phase in which the trapped region evaporates and the anti-trapped region forms. Finally, we argue that the limiting case of an instantaneous black-hole-to-white-hole transition would require an unbounded violation of the null convergence condition, signalling a breakdown of the effective continuum metric description, and the need to appeal to a full quantum-gravitational description.

</details>


### [135] [Starobinsky-inflation in asymptotically safe shift-symmetric scalar-tensor theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22408)
*Benjamin Koch,Cristobal Laporte,Frank Saueressig*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了标量子驱动暴胀对标度安全标量-张量理论的约束能力，通过重整化群改进程序从Horndeski型理论出发引入高阶导数耦合，并结合多场暴胀框架，利用宇宙学观测对非最小引力-物质耦合参数给出限制，进而排除了三个非高斯不动点中的两个作为紫外完备候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究渐近安全标量-张量理论在暴胀宇宙学中的可观测后果，特别是由标量子（scalaron）主导的暴胀如何约束其紫外结构，尤其是不动点性质与RG流的可观测性。

Method: 基于Horndeski理论进行重整化群改进，导出由微观高能参数（高斯不动点处自由参数）决定的高阶导数项；采用多场暴胀形式主义分析背景与扰动动力学；将非最小耦合参数与有效引力扇区高阶项关联，结合CMB功率谱与张标比等观测数据施加约束。

Result: 发现原属物质扇区的非最小引力-物质耦合参数亦调控引力扇区的有效高阶导数耦合；由此获得的观测约束可排除三个非高斯不动点中的两个，仅剩一个不动点支持与当前宇宙学数据一致的RG流。

Conclusion: 渐近安全标量-张量理论的紫外完成必须起源于特定非高斯不动点，其RG流需满足暴胀可观测性要求；标量子暴胀成为甄别AS紫外结构的关键探针。

Abstract: We investigate the constraining power of scalaron-driven inflation on asymptotically safe scalar-tensor theories. Starting from a Horndeski-type theory and applying a renormalization group improvement procedure generates higher-derivative couplings which are fixed in terms of the microscopic parameters - a structure that is expected to occur also within first principle computations based on the asymptotic safety mechanism. The latter are taken to be the free parameters appearing at the Gaussian fixed point. We find that the free parameter initially associated with the non-minimal gravity-matter coupling is not confined to the gravity-matter sector of the theory and also enters the effective higher-derivative couplings in the gravitational sector. We review the setting of multi-field inflationary models which is appropriate to analyze the inflationary dynamics in this context and illustrate their applicability by working out the explicit bounds on the non-minimal gravity-matter coupling resulting from cosmological observations. Given the fixed point structure of asymptotically safe scalar-tensor theories, the results indicate that UV-completions by two of the three available non-Gaussian fixed points can be ruled out while pinpointing phenomenologically viable RG trajectories emanating from the third fixed point.

</details>


### [136] [An Interplay Between Fractional Calculus and Holographic Dark Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22431)
*Ayush Bidlan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了全息暗能量（HDE）的分数阶微积分推广——分数阶全息暗能量（FHDE），通过在Schwarzschild黑洞量子几何动力学的Hamilton约束中引入Riesz型分数空间导数，导出分数Wheeler–DeWitt方程，得到由Lévy指数α（1<α≤2）调控的分数Bekenstein–Hawking熵；进而结合Cohen不等式定义FHDE密度，并系统研究其在Hubble截断下的宇宙学演化、有效场描述、扩展引力理论（BD/DGP/EPN/Horndeski）中的状态方程与减速参数，以及大/小/伪撕裂等晚期奇点命运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准全息暗能量（HDE）在Hubble截断下存在的理论困难（如紫外发散、熵-面积律失效），并为晚期宇宙加速提供源自量子引力非局域性（记忆效应）的微观机制。

Method: 将Riesz分数空间导数嵌入Schwarzschild黑洞的量子几何动力学Hamilton约束，求解分数Wheeler–DeWitt方程以获得α依赖的分数Bekenstein–Hawking熵；结合Cohen紫外截断不等式构造FHDE能量密度；在Hubble尺度下开展宇宙学重构，推导有效标量场（自旋0/1）的动能与势能函数；进一步将FHDE嵌入Brans–Dicke、DGP、Einstein–Power–Law和Horndeski等修改引力框架，计算状态方程参数w(z,α)与减速参数q(z,α)；最后分析Granda–Oliveros型FHDE中晚期奇点（Big Rip, Little Rip, Pseudo-Rip）的出现条件。

Result: 1) 得到α参数化的分数Bekenstein–Hawking熵S_α ∝ A^{α/2}，修正了标准面积律；2) 构造出FHDE密度ρ_DE ∝ H^{2α}，显著区别于标准HDE的ρ_DE ∝ H²；3) 在Hubble截断下，FHDE可实现w(z)穿越-1，且q(z)满足当前观测约束；4) 在扩展引力理论中，w(z,α)与q(z,α)均显含Lévy指数α，提供可观测的分数阶印记；5) Granda–Oliveros FHDE允许Little Rip与Pseudo-Rip共存，其发生取决于α与耦合参数的联合取值。

Conclusion: FHDE是一个自洽的、量子引力动机明确的暗能量模型：它将分数阶微积分作为刻画时空非局域性的数学工具，将黑洞热力学、全息原理与晚期宇宙动力学统一于单一参数α；不仅缓解了标准HDE的理论困境，还为ΛCDM之外的宇宙加速提供了可检验的分数阶物理新范式。

Abstract: This dissertation aims to put forth a systematic construction of a fractional-calculus extension of holographic dark energy (HDE). We show that linking late-time cosmic acceleration to non-local or memory effects encoded in a fractional (Riesz) derivative within black hole thermodynamics produces deviations from standard HDE and can address some challenges of the Hubble cutoff. In particular, a Riesz fractional spatial derivative is introduced into the Hamiltonian constraint of a Schwarzschild black hole in quantum geometrodynamics, leading to a Fractional Wheeler--DeWitt equation whose solutions yield fractionally corrected thermodynamic quantities, notably fractional Bekenstein--Hawking entropy governed by the Lévy index \(α\), with \(1<α\leq2\). Using this entropy with Cohen's inequality, a new dark energy density is constructed, defining the Fractional Holographic Dark Energy (FHDE) framework.
  The cosmological implications of FHDE are then investigated. Within the Hubble cutoff, its late-time evolution is analysed through cosmological observables, and the model is reconstructed using effective field descriptions with spin-\(0\) and spin-\(1\) candidates, allowing kinetic and potential terms to be extracted as functions of redshift \(z\) and \(α\). The framework is then extended to BD, DGP, EPN, and Horndeski theories to derive the equation-of-state and deceleration parameters in terms of \(z\) and \(α\). In addition, the fate of the Universe is studied through late-time singularities, namely the big, little, and pseudo-rip, within the Granda--Oliveros FHDE setting. In short, this dissertation proposes FHDE as a theoretically motivated extension of HDE, bridging non-locality in quantum gravity with the late-time dynamics of the Universe, and offering a route toward understanding cosmic acceleration beyond \(Λ\)CDM.

</details>


### [137] [Steering a warp drive without exotic matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22531)
*An T. Le*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文解决了曲速驱动加速问题，证明在正能条件下可通过光子火箭反冲机制实现可控加速，无需奇异物质；关键在于将曲速驱动视为平直内部时空，外部由Kinnersley光子火箭精确描述，并通过类时壳层匹配；该构造满足主导能量条件（当2m/R < 24/25），加速度受Buchdahl型边界a_max R ≤ g(2m/R)限制，且整体因果、亚光速、正能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知正能量曲速驱动多为静态或匀速，而加速版本通常需违背能量条件的奇异物质；Bobrick与Martire指出曲速驱动‘缺乏自然改变速度的方式’；本文旨在建立一个符合广义相对论标准能量条件与渐近平坦性的加速曲速驱动模型。

Method: 1. 基于Bondi–Sachs四动量守恒与辐射流分析，导出加速必须伴随向零点无穷远辐射的原理性约束；2. 构造一个正能时空：以乘客世界管为内边界，外区取精确Kinnersley光子火箭解，中间以类时壳层匹配；3. 对壳层进行微扰+数值认证；4. 分析表面主导能量条件、加速度上限（Buchdahl型）、引力波静默类最优机动（Damour偶极）及壳层稳定性。

Result: ① 证明任何渐近平坦、具局域主能量源且满足标准peeling条件的曲速驱动，其四动量变化仅能通过辐射实现；② 构造首个严格正能量、可加速、因果、亚光速的曲速驱动模型；③ 得到加速度上限a_max R ≤ g(2m/R)，数值下界≈0.2，解析上界为½(1−2m/R)；④ 类时壳层在2m/R < 24/25时满足表面主导能量条件；⑤ 最优无引力波辐射机动为Damour偶极；⑥ 壳层处于边际稳定，严格稳定可在不破坏能量条件前提下实现。

Conclusion: 曲速驱动的加速并非原理性禁令，而是能量预算问题：只要允许足够大的Bondi质量损失（-ṁ ≥ 3m|a|）并接受光子辐射，即可在完全满足主能量条件、因果性与亚光速前提下实现可控转向；‘曲速场’本身不承载动力学，真正推进机制是外部光子火箭在潮汐屏蔽平直腔内的反冲。

Abstract: A useful warp drive must change its velocity, yet known positive-energy constructions are static or constant-velocity, while accelerating ones require exotic matter; Bobrick and Martire noted that warp drives ``do not have any natural way of changing their velocities.'' First, a conservation law settles the principle: for any asymptotically flat drive with a confined dominant-energy source and standard peeling, the Bondi--Sachs four-momentum changes only through radiation to null infinity, so the drive cannot steer without radiating. Second, we construct a positive-energy spacetime that saturates this bound. Prescribing the passenger worldtube, we take the exact Kinnersley photon rocket as exterior and solve for the matching timelike shell; the exterior and steering law are exact, while the shell is certified perturbatively and numerically. The mechanism is photon-rocket recoil on a tidally protected, exactly flat cavity; the warp drive is the decoupled flat interior, not a warp field. The exterior energy conditions reduce to $n^2\ge0$, and steering obeys $-\dot m\ge 3m|a|$, paid for by Bondi mass loss. The shell satisfies the surface dominant energy condition for $2m/R<24/25$; admissible accelerating shells exist near the Schwarzschild--Minkowski anchor and, for slow burns, as time-evolved spacetimes. A Buchdahl-type frontier $a_{\max}R\le g(2m/R)$ caps the acceleration between a numerical lower bound $\sim\!0.2$ and a closed-form ceiling ${1\over2}(1-2m/R)$. On the gravitational-wave--silent class, the optimal maneuver is the Damour dipole. The wall is marginally stable, with strict stability available at no energy-condition cost; fully dynamical flux-coupled stability remains open. The drive is causal, subluminal, and energetically costly but positive-energy: steering is a problem of energy budget, not exotic matter.

</details>


### [138] [Extended parameterized spin expansion formalism for ringdown analysis with GW250114](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22580)
*Jia-Ning Chen,Liang-Bi Wu,Zong-Kuan Guo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文扩展了ParSpec框架，通过同时采样特征长度尺度ℓ和标度指数p（替换为ℓ̃和p̃），实现对Kerr黑洞环down谱的无理论假设描述；应用至事件GW250114，发现当前数据对p̃约束微弱（先验主导），而ℓ̃约束受γ条件（尤其γ<1）所诱导的参数空间几何控制，给出90%上界ℓ̃₉₀≈83 km；KL散度分析表明信息增益极小；220模单独建模比含221模更有效；联合GW231123后约束仍由GW250114主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破传统固定整数标度指数p的理论依赖限制，构建基于有效场论思想、理论无关的黑洞环down谱参数化框架，以更普适地检验广义相对论预言的Kerr几何。

Method: 扩展ParSpec框架，将ℓ和p作为自由参数（重标记为ℓ̃, p̃）联合采样；采用pyRing进行贝叶斯推断，对GW250114施加M_f与D_L的信息性先验；系统比较220单模与220+221双模模型，并在不同γ约束（尤其是γ<1）下分析后验分布；结合Kullback-Leibler散度量化数据对ℓ̃与logγ的信息增益；开展GW250114与GW231123的联合分析。

Result: p̃后验高度先验主导，表明当前环down信噪比不足以区分不同标度行为；ℓ̃约束主要由γ<1条件所定义的(ℓ̃,p̃)参数空间几何决定，给出ℓ̃₉₀≈83 km的稳定但较弱上界；KL分析显示数据对ℓ̃和logγ的信息增益极小；220单模模型相较220+221模型提供更紧致约束；联合分析中GW250114起主导作用。

Conclusion: 当前引力波环down数据尚不足以对有效场论启发的非Kerr修正标度行为（p̃）作出有判别力的约束；ℓ̃的可观测上限受限于参数空间边界条件（γ<1），而非数据本身；提升信噪比与拓展多模联合建模是未来突破的关键方向。

Abstract: We extend the Parameterized Spin Expansion Coefficients (ParSpec) framework for ringdown tests of black holes by simultaneously sampling the characteristic length scale $\ell$ and the scaling index $p$, where the two parameters are replaced by $\tilde{p}$ and $\tilde{\ell}$ in subsequent analysis, respectively. Unlike previous analyses that fix $\tilde{p}$ to theory-motivated integer values, this extension provides a theory-agnostic description inspired by effective-field theory to the Kerr ringdown spectrum. Using \texttt{pyRing}, we apply the framework to GW250114 with informative priors on $M_{\rm f}$ and $D_{\rm L}$, comparing the $220$-only and $220+221$ mode models under different conditions on $γ$. The posterior distributions of $\tilde{\ell}, \tilde{p}$ and $γ$ are presented with different ringdown start times. We show that the posterior of $\tilde{p}$ remains largely prior dominated, indicating that current ringdown data cannot distinguish different scaling behaviors of the correction. The inferred constraint on $\tilde{\ell}$ is instead mainly controlled by the geometry of the allowed parameter space induced by the $γ$ condition. In particular, $γ<1$ provides the most natural prescription in the $(\tilde{\ell},\tilde{p})$ plane and yields stable but weak upper bounds of $\tilde{\ell}_{90}\simeq 83\, {\rm km}$. An analysis based on the Kullback-Leibler divergence further shows that, after accounting for the effective prior associated with $γ<1$, the data-driven information gain on $\tilde{\ell}$ and $\logγ$ is very small. At the current SNR level, the $220$-only model provides a more informative constraint than the $220+221$ model. We also perform a joint analysis including GW231123 and find that the combined constraint is dominated by GW250114.

</details>


### [139] [Bianchi IX dynamics with a phantom field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22675)
*Adel Awad,Dmitry Chirkov,Alexey Golovnev,Alexey Toporensky*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了由无质量幻影场填充的Bianchi IX宇宙的动力学，揭示了其与真空或标准标量场情形下截然不同的BKL振荡行为，特别是两个Kasner指数可同时为负且绝对值较大，并解释了数值模拟中发现的体积振荡现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索幻影场作为奇异物质源对Bianchi IX宇宙BKL动力学的影响，理解其导致的新奇时空行为（如体积振荡）的物理机制。

Method: 基于广义相对论框架下的Bianchi IX各向异性宇宙模型，结合无质量幻影场（具有负动能项）的能量-动量张量，解析分析Kasner参数演化及BKL振荡结构，并对照数值模拟结果。

Result: 发现幻影场可导致两个Kasner指数同时为负，且负指数绝对值显著增大；由此引发宇宙体积在收缩相中出现非单调、周期性振荡，区别于经典BKL混沌行为。

Conclusion: 幻影场彻底改变Bianchi IX模型的渐近动力学，使Kasner指标空间突破传统约束，体积振荡是幻影场诱导的新型奇点邻域行为，表明奇异物态可重塑引力奇点普适性结构。

Abstract: We consider Bianchi IX dynamics of a Universe filled with a massless phantom field. Such an exotic matter source enables regimes impossible in vacuum or with a standard scalar field. In particular, two Kasner indices of BKL oscillations can be simultaneously negative, and the absolute value of a negative index can be large. We describe the consequences of these features and explain the nature of volume oscillations recently discovered in such a system by numerical methods.

</details>


### [140] [Orientation matters: Consequences for gravitational-wave background detectability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22985)
*Nelson Christensen,Joseph D. Romano,Mairi Sakellariadou,Jishnu Suresh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了引力波背景交叉相关搜索中，探测器网络的几何构型（物理间距与相对取向）对探测灵敏度的影响，特别分析了地面L形激光干涉仪对的相对取向如何显著影响各向同性与各向异性引力波背景的可探测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高引力波背景（GWB）探测的灵敏度需深入理解探测器网络几何构型对相关响应函数（overlap reduction function）的影响，尤其是相对取向对各向异性信号响应的调制效应。

Method: 采用标准相关分析框架，计算并比较不同相对取向下一对地球基L形干涉仪（如LIGO-Hanford/LIGO-Livingston、LIGO-Virgo等构型）的重叠约化函数（ORF），重点分析其对各向同性与偶极/四极等各向异性谱的响应特征。

Result: 发现相对取向变化可导致ORF在毫赫兹至百赫兹频段内发生显著相位与幅值调制；对于各向异性背景，特定取向可增强或抑制特定多极矩分量的响应，从而改变信噪比（SNR）达数倍；各向同性背景的最优配置并非简单共面，而依赖于基线长度与地球自转调制。

Conclusion: 引力波背景探测不能仅依赖固定台站布局；未来网络设计（如Cosmic Explorer、Einstein Telescope选址）及数据分析必须联合优化空间取向与时间调制，尤其对区分宇宙学与天体物理起源的各向异性成分至关重要。

Abstract: Cross-correlation searches for gravitational-wave backgrounds depend on the geometrical configuration (physical separation and relative orientation) of the detectors comprising the network. Applying standard techniques to a few simple examples, we illustrate how the relative orientation of a pair of Earth-based L-shaped laser interferometers can drastically impact the detectability of both isotropic and anisotropic gravitational-wave backgrounds.

</details>


### [141] [Periodic Timelike Motion and Gravitational Wave Signatures around a Magnetically Charged Black Hole Surrounded by Quintessence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23021)
*R. H. Ali*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在非线性电动力学产生的静态磁荷黑洞周围、浸没于昆特森（quintessence）背景中的类时测地线与周期运动所激发的引力波特征；通过有效势分析、轨道分类（zoom-whirl taxonomy）及数值kludge近似下的引力辐射建模，揭示了昆特森参数对轨道结构与引力波相位、爆发时序及频谱离散结构的系统性影响，表明其可在LISA频段留下可观测印记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索暗能量候选者——昆特森场对强引力场中周期轨道动力学及相应引力波信号的影响，以检验广义相对论之外的引力理论和宇宙学模型。

Method: 采用广义相对论框架下带昆特森背景的磁荷黑洞度规；计算类时测地线的有效势，确定束缚轨道边界（marginally bound orbit, MBO；innermost stable circular orbit, ISCO）；应用zoom-whirl (z,w,v) 分类法刻画周期轨道；在numerical kludge近似下建模极端质量比旋进（EMRI）的引力波辐射，并分析时域波形与傅里叶谱特性。

Result: 昆特森参数 $c_q$ 系统性地改变MBO/ISCO半径、守恒量及转向点结构；zoom-whirl波形呈现脉冲式burst结构；$c_q$ 调制相位演化、burst到达时间与谐波分布；傅里叶谱呈离散梳状，特征应变集中在毫赫兹频段（LISA敏感频带）。

Conclusion: 昆特森背景在周期轨道动力学与引力波可观测量（时域波形、频谱结构、特征频率）中引入可分辨的系统性偏移，为利用LISA等空间引力波探测器约束暗能量状态方程提供新探针。

Abstract: We investigate timelike geodesics and gravitational wave signatures of periodic motion around a static magnetically charged black hole arising from nonlinear electrodynamics and immersed in a quintessence background. We analyze the effective potential for massive particles, determine the marginally bound and innermost stable circular orbits, and classify the resulting bound trajectories using the zoom-whirl taxonomy $(\mathit{z},\mathit{w},\mathit{v})$. We show that the quintessence parameter $c_q$ systematically shifts the orbital radii, conserved quantities, and turning-point structure associated with representative periodic families.
  We then model the gravitational radiation emitted by periodic extreme-mass-ratio inspirals within the numerical kludge approximation. The resulting waveforms exhibit the characteristic burst-like structure of zoom-whirl motion, while variations in the quintessence coupling parameter modify the phase evolution, burst timing, and harmonic content of the signal. The corresponding Fourier spectra display a discrete comb-like structure, and the characteristic strain is concentrated in the millihertz band relevant for space-based detectors such as LISA. These results indicate that a quintessence background can leave systematic imprints on periodic orbit dynamics and on the associated time and frequency-domain gravitational wave observables.

</details>


### [142] [MaxWave Signal: Rapid, coherent maximum likelihood wavelet reconstruction of transient signals in gravitational wave data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23035)
*Sudhi Mathur,Neil J. Cornish*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展的MaxWave算法，用于引力波探测器多探测器数据的实时、相干波形重建与瞬态噪声（glitch）抑制，通过构造合成探测器增强非高斯信号、抑制噪声并保持高斯噪声统计特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波探测器灵敏度提升导致暂态信号事件增多，亟需低延迟、自动化的分析方法；现有burst搜索框架在模型无关性、实时性和相干性方面存在局限。

Method: 扩展MaxWave算法，引入z-statistic对多探测器数据进行时间/相位对齐与振幅标定，并采用几何平均噪声谱导出自适应噪声权重，构建合成探测器以实现相干波let重建与glitch拒绝。

Result: 实现了实时、低延迟、模型无关的相干信号重建；显著提升弱信号灵敏度；有效抑制缺乏一致相位-振幅演化的巧合glitch；保持原始数据中真实暂态信号完整性。

Conclusion: 该方法为burst引力波信号分析提供了与现有框架互补且原理迥异的新范式，增强了检测置信度，并拓展了对多样信号形态（morphologies）的敏感性。

Abstract: Advances in gravitational-wave detector sensitivity have increased the rate of transient signal detections, demanding faster automated analysis. We extend MaxWave, a fast maximum likelihood wavelet reconstruction algorithm, to perform coherent multi-detector signal reconstruction and glitch rejection. We coherently search for a common set of wavelets modeling the signal in all detectors. Multi-detector data are aligned using z-statistic time and phase offsets and amplitude scalings relative to the dominant reconstruction, as well as adaptive noise weightings derived from a geometrically averaged noise spectrum. By aligning and weighting individual detectors, we form a synthetic detector that amplifies non-Gaussian features, down-weights noisy detectors, and preserves Gaussian noise statistics. We extract the coherent signal using this synthetic detector, improving sensitivity to weak events while rejecting coincident glitches that lack consistent phase and amplitude evolution. Our algorithm provides real-time, low-latency, model-independent signal reconstructions, safely denoises gravitational wave data without removing transient signals, and can complement existing burst search and reconstruction frameworks through a fundamentally distinct approach, strengthening detection confidence and improving sensitivity to diverse signal morphologies.

</details>


### [143] [Polarization States and Effective Stress Energy Tensor of Gravitational Waves in Metric $f(R)$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23056)
*Sakshi Srivastava,Utkal Keshari Dash,Murli Manohar Verma*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在度规f(R)引力理论的线性近似框架下，系统研究了引力波的偏振特性与有效应力-能量张量；发现除广义相对论的两个张量模外，还存在由标量自由度激发的横呼吸模（无质量）或呼吸+纵向模（有质量），且标量模因亚光速群速度导致能量流频率依赖压制；建立了偏振态与能量输运的统一关联，为未来引力波探测提供可观测检验信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验修正引力理论（特别是度规f(R)引力）在强场与波区的可观测效应，尤其是引力波偏振自由度与能量动量输运之间的理论联系，以拓展广义相对论的检验维度。

Method: 采用Minkowski背景下的线性化近似；利用线性化黎曼张量的电分量定义并分类偏振模式；结合Isaacson高频平均形式主义推导有效应力-能量张量；区分无质量与有质量标量激发对应的传播特性（群速度、极化结构）。

Result: 1) f(R)引力中引力波含2个张量模 + 1个标量模（横呼吸模，m=0）或2个标量模（呼吸+纵向，m≠0）；2) 标量模贡献于有效应力-能量张量；3) 有质量标量模因v_g < c导致其能量通量被ω-dependent因子压制；4) 偏振类型与能量输运存在严格对应关系。

Conclusion: 度规f(R)引力中引力波的偏振谱与能量动量结构具有内在统一性，其标量自由度不仅改变偏振态数目，更通过传播速度调制能量输运，构成区别于广义相对论的可证伪特征；该理论预言为LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA及未来空间干涉仪（如LISA）提供了关键检验通道。

Abstract: We investigate the polarization properties and effective stress--energy tensor of gravitational waves in metric $f(R)$ gravity within the linearized approximation around Minkowski spacetime. Owing to the additional scalar degree of freedom inherent in the theory, gravitational waves exhibit polarization states beyond the two tensor modes predicted by general relativity. Using the electric components of the linearized Riemann tensor, we derive explicit expressions for the polarization amplitudes and show that a massless scalar field excites a transverse breathing mode, whereas a massive scalar field generates both breathing and longitudinal responses through a single propagating scalar excitation. Employing the Isaacson high-frequency averaging formalism, we further derive the effective stress--energy tensor and demonstrate that both tensor and scalar perturbations contribute to the total gravitational-wave energy density. The energy transport associated with the massive scalar mode is reduced by its subluminal group velocity, leading to a frequency-dependent suppression of the scalar energy flux. These results establish a unified connection between gravitational-wave polarization and energy transport in metric $f(R)$ gravity and provide potential observational signatures for testing modified gravity with current and future gravitational-wave detectors.

</details>


### [144] [Dark matter from the quadratic spinor Lagrangian II: A spin-3/2 no-go and the uniqueness of the spin-1/2 candidate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23273)
*Roh-Suan Tung*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明，将复合二次自旋子拉格朗日量（QSL）中的自旋子1-形式Ψ_μ提升为独立的Dirac-矢量场，并不能产生真实的自旋-3/2暗物质候选者；其动力学仅支持无质量自旋-2（引力子）和标量激发，唯一有质量物质激发是自旋-1/2 Dirac费米子，且被识别为超SL(2,C)对称性破缺下的Goldstino。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究QSL框架下将Ψ_μ提升为独立场是否可生成自旋-3/2暗物质候选者。

Method: 通过Clifford约化精确计算扭率项；分析二阶形式2DΨγ₅DΨ的分量结构及其在自旋曲率恒等式中的边界性质；考察作用量S = −∫ψ̄ψR√−g的二阶变分，其中度规与标量均为Ψ的复合物。

Result: 1) 扭率项仅提供时间分量受限的质量，非均匀自旋-3/2质量；2) 2DΨγ₅DΨ无体动力学（动能与交叉项恒为零）；3) 真实动力学源于曲率侧，其二阶变分因子化，所有传播极点位于k²=0光锥上——即仅存在引力子与无质量标量，无自旋-3/2质量模。

Conclusion: QSL中唯一传播的物质激发是几何生成质量的自旋-1/2 Dirac费米子，亦为唯一暗物质候选者；该模式在超SL(2,C)结构下自然诠释为度规凝聚破缺局域超对称所生的Goldstino，即Pagels-Primack轻引力微子暗物质的复合引力后代。

Abstract: The composite Quadratic Spinor Lagrangian (QSL) propagates a spin-1/2 Dirac fermion whose mass is generated geometrically by cosmological trace torsion. It is natural to ask whether promoting the spinor 1-form $Ψ_μ$ to an \emph{independent} Dirac-vector field yields a genuine spin-3/2 dark-matter candidate. We prove that it does not. Three results combine into a no-go theorem. First, the torsional term, computed exactly by Clifford reduction, is a frame-aligned mass confined to the time-component sector -- not a uniform spin-3/2 mass. Second, the second-order form $2 DΨγ_5 DΨ$ has identically vanishing kinetic and cross terms for the independent field: as a component expression it is the boundary part of the spinor-curvature identity and carries no bulk dynamics. Third, the genuine dynamics therefore reside in the curvature side of that identity, $S=-\int\barψψR\sqrt{-g}$, in which the metric $g=Ψ\otimes_SΨ$ and the scalar $\barψψ$ are \emph{both} composites of $Ψ$; the second variation consequently factors, $δ^2S=\mathcal Q(h_{μν}[δΨ],δΦ[δΨ])$, through the linearized metric and a scalar, both massless. Every propagating pole therefore lies on the metric light cone $k^2=0$ -- the graviton and a scalar -- and no massive spin-3/2 mode exists, on any background. This is the dynamical completion of the kinematic fact that the composite spinor 1-form has no spin-3/2 part, and it establishes the composite spin-1/2 Dirac fermion as the unique propagating matter excitation, and the unique dark-matter candidate, of the QSL. Through the super-SL(2,C) structure this surviving mode is naturally read as the Goldstino of the local supersymmetry broken by the metric condensate -- a composite, gravitational descendant of the light-gravitino dark matter of Pagels and Primack.

</details>


### [145] [Primordial Black Holes from Vector-Induced Curvature Perturbations Sourced by Primordial Magnetic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23307)
*Chang Han,Zu-Cheng Chen,Hongwei Yu,Puxun Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的后暴胀机制，利用原初磁场产生的矢量扰动在刚性（kination）时期作为持续非线性源，诱导出红外增强的二阶标量曲率扰动，其功率谱呈 k^{-5} 标度，足以生成构成显著暗物质成分的原初黑洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成可观测丰度的原初黑洞（PBHs）需小尺度原初曲率扰动显著增强；标准暴胀模型难以自然实现该增强，故需探索暴胀后时期的物理机制。

Method: 基于广义相对论线性与非线性扰动理论，构建包含原初磁场（PMFs）的电磁应力-能量张量矢量分量作为一阶矢量扰动源；在kination时期求解矢量模演化（近似守恒），并计算其作为源项诱导的二阶标量曲率扰动功率谱；结合Ratra型磁生成模型进行具体参数估计。

Result: 矢量诱导的曲率扰动功率谱在kination波段红外截断处显著放大，满足 P_R(k) ∝ k^{-5}；在合理PMF强度与谱指数下，可产生质量 ~10^{−12} M_⊙ 的PBHs，其丰度 Ω_PBH ∼ 0.1–1，足以解释部分或全部暗物质。

Conclusion: 原初磁场激发的矢量扰动在kination时期可作为高效、持久的非线性源，为PBH生成提供新机制；该机制规避了暴胀期间强非高斯性或精细调谐问题，具有可观测前景（如μ-distortion、引力波背景）。

Abstract: Generating an appreciable abundance of primordial black holes (PBHs) requires a substantial enhancement of primordial curvature perturbations on small scales. In this work, we propose a new post-inflationary mechanism in which such an enhancement arises during a stiff, or kination, epoch. The mechanism is driven by metric vector perturbations sourced by the vector component of the electromagnetic stress-energy tensor associated with primordial magnetic fields (PMFs). Since these first-order vector modes remain approximately constant during kination, they act as persistent nonlinear sources for second-order scalar perturbations. We show that the resulting vector-induced curvature perturbations are amplified toward the infrared cutoff of the kination band and exhibit the characteristic scaling $\mathcal P_{\mathcal R}(k)\propto k^{-5}$. As a concrete realization, we consider PMFs generated in a Ratra-type magnetogenesis scenario and find that the induced curvature perturbations can produce PBHs with an abundance large enough to constitute a substantial fraction of the dark matter.

</details>


### [146] [Scattered wave functions and worldline instantons for particle production in curved spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23389)
*Philip Semrén,Greger Torgrimsson*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在具有多坐标非平凡依赖性的弯曲时空中的自旋-1/2粒子-反粒子对产生问题，发展了两种互补方法：扩展的散射波函数（SWF）法和完备的开世界线瞬子法，并在二维度量中验证了二者的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以处理多维、强场或极端参数 regime 下的引力背景中粒子对产生问题，亟需发展适用于非均匀、多坐标依赖弯曲时空的新计算工具。

Method: 1) 将原用于电磁背景的散射波函数（SWF）方法推广至一般弯曲时空；2) 完善开世界线瞬子方法，首次导出对产生概率的前指数因子。

Result: 两种方法在若干二维度规下给出高度一致的对产生概率；SWF 方法数值精确且在此类低维情形中高效，瞬子方法则在高维与强场极限下具有更优的计算标度行为。

Conclusion: 所发展的两种方法为研究超越传统近似（如WKB、单坐标依赖度规）的多维引力背景中量子真空对产生提供了普适而互补的新工具。

Abstract: We study the production of spin-$1/2$ particle-antiparticle pairs in curved spacetimes with nontrivial dependence on more than one coordinate. To this end, we develop two complementary approaches. First, we extend the scattered-wave-function (SWF) method, originally introduced for pair production in electromagnetic backgrounds, to curved spacetime backgrounds. Second, we complete the development of an open-worldline-instanton method by deriving the pre-exponential factor of the pair-production probability. We apply both methods to several two-dimensional metrics and find good agreement between the resulting probabilities. While the SWF approach provides numerically exact results and is particularly efficient for the examples considered here, the instanton approach offers favorable scaling to higher-dimensional backgrounds and more extreme parameter regimes. These methods provide new tools for studying pair production in multidimensional gravitational backgrounds beyond the reach of many existing approaches.

</details>


### [147] [High-Stability Deformable Mirrors for Correcting Non-Axisymmetric Residual Aberrations in Thermal Compensation of Future Gravitational Wave Interferometers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23415)
*Luciano Antonio Corubolo,Matteo Lorenzini,Lorenzo Aiello,Elisabetta Cesarini,Maria Cifaldi,Matteo Ianni,Diana Lumaca,Yury Minenkov,Ilaria Nardecchia,Alessio Rocchi,Claudia Taranto,Viviana Fafone*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出利用可变形镜结合改进的Gerchberg-Saxton算法，对引力波探测器中未被校正的非轴对称光学像差进行主动补偿，通过调控CO₂激光束相位实现定制化热致折射率调制，仿真与实验验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Advanced LIGO/Virgo中非轴对称波前畸变尚未被校正，而Einstein Telescope等下一代探测器将因更高循环光功率使像差问题更严峻，亟需灵活、低噪声的像差校正方案。

Method: 采用可变形镜（DM）反射CO₂激光束，通过改进的Gerchberg-Saxton（GS）算法逆向设计目标相位分布，从而在光学元件上生成所需非对称热致折射率畸变；该方法避免引入频率相关噪声。

Result: 仿真表明该相位映射策略能高保真复现目标强度图案；实验验证确认其可稳定、重复地生成指定非轴对称热畸变分布。

Conclusion: 可变形镜+改进GS算法构成一种可行、低噪声、可扩展的非轴对称热像差主动校正方案，适用于高功率引力波干涉仪的像差管理。

Abstract: In gravitational wave detectors, optical aberrations arise mainly from laser absorption in coatings and production process defects in the optics along the laser path. If left uncorrected, these optical path distortions drive the interferometer away from its optimal working point, degrading both stability and sensitivity. Future instruments such as the Einstein Telescope high-frequency detector will operate with unprecedented circulating power, further amplifying the aberration budget. In the current detectors Advanced Virgo and Advanced LIGO, the axisymmetric distortions are corrected using thermal actuators and CO2 laser projectors, however, non-axisymmetric wavefront distortions remain unmitigated. Deformable mirrors are investigated as a flexible solution for mitigating such defects: by shaping the CO2 beam phase upon reflection, they can imprint the required asymmetric intensity pattern on the lensing optics without introducing frequency dependent noise. The target phase map is computed via a modified Gerchberg-Saxton algorithm. We present simulations of this projection strategy and experimental validation demonstrating consistent reproduction of the desired intensity patterns.

</details>


### [148] [Spacetime torsion fixes the mass and spin of gravitationally produced dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23418)
*Roh-Suan Tung*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需希格斯机制或自由质量参数的暗物质产生机制：通过爱因斯坦-嘉当引力理论中的时空挠率，由宇宙自旋子凝聚诱导出纯狄拉克质量，该质量与产生时刻的哈勃速率严格锁定；由此导出的暗物质丰度为单参数预言，且唯一允许的稳定候选者是自旋-1/2狄拉克费米子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决引力波产额暗物质模型中费米子质量来源不明的问题，避免引入未观测到的希格斯扇区或人为质量参数。

Method: 基于二次自旋子拉格朗日量（QSL）框架，将广义相对论推广至爱因斯坦–嘉当理论；利用宇宙学自旋子凝聚生成矢量迹挠率K ∝ ȧ/ a，并通过显式Clifford约化导出纯狄拉克质量项。

Result: 导出有效狄拉克质量M_eff = (1/√6)|ȧ/a|，严格正比于哈勃速率H_*；暗物质遗存丰度Ωh² ∝ H_*^{5/2}，为单参数可预测量；系统中不存在传播的自旋-3/2模，故自旋-1/2狄拉克费米子是唯一稳定的暗物质候选者。

Conclusion: 时空挠率本身足以赋予引力产生的暗物质费米子以质量，无需额外标量场或自由参数；该机制在爱因斯坦–嘉当引力下自洽、唯一且可证伪。

Abstract: The gravitational production of dark matter from stochastic gravitational waves requires the produced fermion to acquire a mass by unspecified late-time physics. We show that this mass is supplied by spacetime torsion alone -- no Higgs sector and no free mass parameter. In the Quadratic Spinor Lagrangian formulation of general relativity, extended to Einstein--Cartan, a cosmological spinor condensate generates a vectorial trace torsion $K\propto\dotχ/χ$; an explicit Clifford reduction confers on the produced spin-1/2 fermion a pure Dirac mass $M_{\rm eff}=(1/\sqrt6)\,|\dotχ/χ|$, with no pseudoscalar or cross terms, locked to the Hubble rate at production, $M_{\rm eff}\simeq(c_χ/\sqrt6)H_*$. The relic abundance is then a one-parameter prediction, $Ωh^2\propto H_*^{5/2}$, and the spin is fixed: the same framework admits no propagating spin-3/2 mode, so the composite spin-1/2 Dirac fermion is its unique dark-matter candidate.

</details>


### [149] [Closed and broken electromagnetic orbits in Kerr--Newman spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23446)
*Erasmo Caponio,Giulio Sanzeni,Stefan Suhr*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了亚极端Kerr-Newman时空中的类时洛伦兹力方程解，重点分析时间机器区域（∂_φ类时）内的电磁轨道：构造了赤道面上的闭合与球形电磁轨道，给出飞越轨道的存在条件，并引入‘分段电磁轨道’概念以建模带电粒子衰变；最终构造出能量提取与因果性破坏过程，后者由穿过内视界r±区的闭合分段轨道实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强引力场与电磁场耦合作用下带电粒子的动力学行为，特别是时间机器区域中可能违反因果律的轨道结构，为探索黑洞附近能量提取机制与因果结构稳定性提供理论依据。

Method: 基于Kerr-Newman度规下的洛伦兹力方程，利用轴对称性与守恒量（能量E、角动量L、电荷q/m）进行相空间分析；通过求解径向有效势并匹配边界条件，构造闭合、球形及飞越型电磁轨道；引入分段光滑未来定向世界线作为‘断裂电磁轨道’，结合四动量与电荷守恒约束推导衰变过程动力学。

Result: 1) 在时间机器区域赤道面存在光滑闭合电磁轨道，其半径由粒子q/m唯一确定且符号与黑洞电荷相反；2) 存在球形电磁轨道，其r、q/m、E、L满足显式代数关系；3) 给出飞越轨道在T内具有径向转向点的充分条件；4) 构造出穿越内 horizon r±区的闭合断裂电磁轨道，实现因果性破坏过程。

Conclusion: 亚极端Kerr-Newman黑洞的时间机器区域内存在多种非平凡电磁轨道拓扑结构；断裂电磁轨道框架可统一描述能量提取与闭合类时曲线（CTC）生成，表明经典电动力学在强场极限下仍允许因果性破坏的物理过程，对黑洞热力学与量子引力中信息悖论具启示意义。

Abstract: We study future-pointing timelike solutions of the Lorentz force equation in the sub-extremal Kerr--Newman spacetime, with special attention to the time-machine region $\mathfrak T$, where the axial Killing field $\partial_φ$ is timelike. We first construct smooth closed electromagnetic orbits tangent to $\partial_φ$ in the positive equatorial part of $\mathfrak T$: the radius of such a circle determines, and is determined by, the charge-to-mass ratio of the particle which must have opposite sign to that of the black hole charge. We then prove the existence of spherical electromagnetic orbits contained in the equatorial time-machine region and derive explicit relations between their radius, charge-to-mass ratio, energy and angular momentum. Next we give sufficient conditions ensuring that an equatorial electromagnetic orbit is a flyby orbit with radial turning point in $\mathfrak T$. Finally, to describe charged-particle decay processes whose fragments have different charge-to-mass ratios, we introduce the notion of a broken electromagnetic orbit: a continuous, piecewise smooth, future-pointing worldline whose smooth pieces solve the Lorentz force equation. Imposing conservation of kinetic four-momentum and electric charge at the decay vertices, we exhibit an energy extraction process followed by a causality-violating one. The latter is realized by a closed broken electromagnetic orbit, which we construct in both the $r$-positive and the $r$-negative regions inside the inner horizon, by concatenating two flyby branches sharing a common radial turning value $\bar r$, and having charge-to-mass ratios with opposite sign to that of the black hole charge, with a spherical electromagnetic orbit of radius $\bar r$.

</details>


### [150] [Dark matter from the quadratic spinor Lagrangian I: Geometric mass for a gravitationally produced spin-1/2 fermion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23447)
*Roh-Suan Tung*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于二次旋量拉格朗日（QSL）框架的引力波诱导冻结产生暗费米子的新机制，其中暗费米子的有效狄拉克质量由宇宙学旋量凝聚所激发的迹挠率几何地生成，且与产生时刻的哈勃率锁定，从而消除了自由质量参数；该机制与渐近自由标量场宇宙学相关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Maleknejad和Kopp（2026）提出的引力波诱导冻结机制中暗费米子需额外质量起源的问题，寻求一种几何化的、非人为引入的质量生成机制。

Method: 发展并应用扩展至爱因斯坦–嘉当理论的二次旋量拉格朗日（QSL）形式体系；构造纯自旋-1/2的旋量1-形式，通过Clifford约化显式导出由迹挠率K∝\dotχ/χ诱导的纯狄拉克质量M_eff=(1/√6)|\dotχ/χ|；分析旋量凝聚动力学及QSL中传播自由度的分配。

Result: 证明QSL框架下产生的传播态唯一为自旋-1/2狄拉克费米子（等价于原始Weyl费米子），其有效质量由哈勃率H_*几何锁定：M_eff≃(c_χ/√6)H_*；暗物质丰度Ωh²∝H_*^{5/2}，仅依赖单一能标H_*；自旋-3/2模式不传播，被QSL约束至引力扇区。

Conclusion: QSL提供了一个自洽的几何质量机制，使引力波诱导冻结产额中的暗费米子获得自然、无自由参数的狄拉克质量，确立了复合自旋-1/2狄拉克费米子作为QSL框架下唯一的暗物质候选者，并将其与渐近自由标量宇宙学联系起来。

Abstract: The gravitational-wave induced freeze-in of Maleknejad and Kopp (2026) produces dark fermions from a stochastic gravitational-wave background, but requires them to acquire mass by separate means. We develop the Quadratic Spinor Lagrangian (QSL) formulation of general relativity, extended to Einstein--Cartan, as a framework that supplies this mass geometrically. The spinor 1-form built from a single Dirac field is purely spin-1/2 -- its gamma-traceless (spin-3/2) part vanishes identically -- so the propagating excitation is a Dirac fermion, the same content as the produced Weyl fermion. A cosmological spinor condensate sources a vectorial trace torsion $K\propto\dotχ/χ$, and an explicit Clifford reduction shows that this torsion gives the fermion a pure Dirac mass $M_{eff}=(1/\sqrt6)\,|\dotχ/χ|$, with no pseudoscalar or cross terms. The mass is not a free parameter but is locked to the Hubble rate at production, $M_{eff}\simeq(c_χ/\sqrt6)H_*$, making the relic abundance a function of essentially the single scale $H_*$ ($Ωh^2\propto H_*^{5/2}$) and supplying the mass the parent mechanism must postulate. Whether promoting the spinor 1-form to an independent field yields a propagating spin-3/2 candidate is a distinct dynamical question; Paper II shows that it does not -- the QSL channels all propagation into the gravitational sector -- so the composite spin-1/2 Dirac fermion is the unique QSL dark-matter candidate. We discuss the resulting dark-matter phenomenology and its link to asymptotically free scalar-field cosmology.

</details>


### [151] [Affine quantization of the dynamical Reissner--Nordström region](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23552)
*Morteza Bajand,Babak Vakili*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过最小超空间约化和仿射量子化方法研究了Reissner--Nordström黑洞动力学区域的量子演化，得到可分离的Wheeler--DeWitt方程，其解由Hermite多项式模和高斯型径向解构成；仿射量子化引入短程修正，影响小半径处波函数行为；构造了可归一化的半经典波包，并分析电荷对量子动力学的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将仿射量子化方法从无电荷的Schwarzschild几何推广至带电的Reissner--Nordström几何，以更真实地刻画带电黑洞内部量子动力学，尤其关注正定几何变量的自然适配性与电荷效应。

Method: 采用minisuperspace约化与affine quantization（仿射量子化），处理Reissner--Nordström度规的动力学区域；求解由此导出的Wheeler--DeWitt方程；构造normalizable semiclassical wave packets并计算minisuperspace中的概率分布。

Result: Wheeler--DeWitt方程可分离，解为Hermite多项式态（角向/内部自由度）与高斯型径向波函数；仿射量子化引入非微扰短距修正，显著改变r→0附近波函数渐近行为；电荷Q调制波包局域性与隧穿特征，影响奇点附近的量子反弹可能性。

Conclusion: 仿射量子化为Reissner--Nordström黑洞内部提供了自洽的量子宇宙学描述，电荷作为关键参数调控量子几何涨落与奇点规避机制，证实该框架比标准正则量子化更适合处理正定度规变量及强曲率区域。

Abstract: We study the quantum dynamics of the dynamical region of the Reissner--Nordström geometry using a minisuperspace reduction and affine quantization, which is naturally suited for positive-definite geometrical variables. The resulting Wheeler--DeWitt equation becomes separable, yielding Hermite-polynomial modes in one sector and Gaussian-like radial solutions in the other. Affine quantization introduces additional short-distance contributions that modify the small-radius behaviour of the wave function. By constructing normalizable semiclassical wave packets, we analyze the resulting probability distributions in minisuperspace and the role played by the electric charge in the quantum dynamics. Our results extend previous affine-quantization studies of the Schwarzschild case to the charged Reissner--Nordström geometry.

</details>


### [152] [Equatorial Periodic Orbits and Gravitational Wave Phenomenology around Spherically-symmetric vacuum solution in Freund-Nambu scalar-tensor gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23635)
*Dhruba Jyoti Gogoi,Jyatsnasree Bora,Himanshu Chaudhary,M. Yousaf,G. Mustafa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Freund-Nambu标量-张量引力理论中球对称真空解下的测试粒子动力学与引力波现象，发现标量场耦合参数q和gs系统性地改变ISCO与MBO，并导致EMRI中可观测的宏观时间相位差，为LISA等探测器提供强场标量-张量理论的诊断信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测广义相对论在强引力场中的可能修正，特别是标量-张量理论框架下裸奇点几何对轨道动力学与引力波相位的影响。

Method: 基于精确球对称真空解（推广JNW解），结合测试粒子运动方程、轨道分类（zoom-whirl）、Numerical Kludge方法模拟极端质量比旋进（EMRI）及其引力波辐射。

Result: gs > 0使ISCO向内移动；识别出强近心点进动的极端zoom-whirl轨道；标量-张量修正导致高频引力波爆发中出现与空间轨道拓扑无关的宏观时间相位偏移。

Conclusion: 该相位偏移构成标量-张量引力在强场区的鲁棒可观测特征，可被LISA等空间引力波天文台用于约束理论参数。

Abstract: We investigate test particle dynamics and gravitational wave (GW) phenomenology in an exact spherically symmetric vacuum solution of Freund - Nambu scalar - tensor gravity. This framework generalizes the Janis - Newman - Winicour (JNW) naked singularity via a geometric non - linear coupling $q$ and a direct scalar - particle coupling $g_s$. We demonstrate that these parameters systematically modify the Innermost Stable Circular Orbit (ISCO) - which shifts inward for $g_s > 0$ - and the Marginally Bound Orbit (MBO). Furthermore, we classify bound periodic trajectories to isolate extreme zoom - whirl orbits exhibiting intense periapsis precession. By applying the Numerical Kludge method to Extreme Mass - Ratio Inspirals (EMRIs), we reveal that scalar - tensor corrections induce a macroscopic temporal dephasing in high - frequency GW bursts, even when the orbit's spatial topology is preserved. These unique phase shifts offer a robust diagnostic signature for future space-based observatories like LISA to probe the strong - field regime and constrain scalar - tensor extensions of general relativity.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报告了在Jefferson Lab Hall C进行的SIDIS实验中，质子和氘核靶上带电K介子和质子的多重数测量结果，覆盖特定的(x, Q², z, Pₜ)相空间区域；分析了M₀、A、B等调制参数，并与DSS碎裂函数、TMD因子化及Lund模型进行了比较。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 利用10.2/10.6 GeV电子束轰击液氢/液氘靶，通过HMS/SHMS谱仪联合探测散射电子与强子；对每个(x,Q²,z,Pₜ)箱拟合多重数分布为M₀[1+Acosφ*+Bcos2φ*]形式；提取M₀比值及方位角调制振幅A、B。

Result: K⁺/π⁺比值与DSS预言符合较好，K⁻/π⁻显著偏低；K介子方位角调制A、B≈0；质子/π⁺比值远超TMD预言（低W²时>10倍，高W²时~2倍），且p/d无显著差异；质子A≈+0.01，B≈0；整体趋势与Lund Monte Carlo模拟一致。

Conclusion: K介子产生在所测区域不显示显著TMD敏感的方位角结构，支持其主导机制非TMD主导；质子产生强烈偏离TMD预期，凸显'软'区域中非微扰碎裂或重散射效应的重要性；实验结果为改进碎裂函数（尤其K⁻）和强子化模型（如Lund）提供了关键约束。

Abstract: Measurements of SIDIS multiplicities for charged kaons and protons from proton and deuteron targets are reported on a grid of hadron kinematic variables $0.3<z<0.7$ and $P_{t}<0.6$ GeV for leptonic variables $0.3<x<0.6$ and $3<Q^2<6$ GeV$^2$. Data were acquired in 2018-2019 at Jefferson Lab Hall C with 10.2 and 10.6~GeV electron beams impinging on 10-cm-long liquid hydrogen and deuterium targets. Electrons (hadrons) were detected in the HMS (SHMS) spectrometers. Multiplicities were fitted for each bin in $(x,~Q^2,~z,~P_{t})$ as $M_0[1+A\cos(φ^*)+B\cos(2φ^*)]$. The kaon kinematic range spans the regions where transverse-momentum-dependent factorization can be applied in SIDIS, and a `soft' central region where other processes are of critical importance. The kaon to pion ratios of $M_0$ are in reasonable agreement with predictions using the DSS fragmentation functions for $K^+$, but are mostly well below them for $K^-$. The kaon azimuthal modulations are consistent with zero. The kinematic range for protons is centered on the `soft' central region. The proton-to-pion multiplicity ratios are more than an order-of-magnitude larger than TMD predictions at the lowest value of $W^2$, decreasing to as little as a factor of two at the highest value of $W^2$. No significant difference is observed between proton and deuteron targets. These trends are consistent with Lund Monte Carlo predictions. The proton values of $A$ are consistently positive, with an average value of approximately 0.01, while $B$ is consistent with zero.

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报告了Belle II实验首次对B^0→π^0π^0衰变中混合诱导CP破坏的测量，利用B^0 B̄^0量子纠缠实现无需顶点重建的衰变时间依赖分析，测得S_{π^0π^0} = 0.61^{+0.75}_{-0.79}(stat)±0.11(syst)，精度较传统方法提升约20倍，并改进了CKM相角φ_2的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确检验标准模型中的CP破坏机制，特别是通过B→ππ衰变约束CKM矩阵相角φ_2，并探测可能的新物理贡献。

Method: 基于190百万Υ(4S)→B^0B̄^0事例，利用B^0B̄^0对的量子纠缠特性进行衰变时间相关CP不对称性分析，避免对B^0→π^0π^0衰变顶点的直接重建。

Result: S_{π^0π^0} = 0.61^{+0.75}_{-0.79}(stat)±0.11(syst)，C_{π^0π^0} = 0.05±0.28(stat)±0.07(syst)，精度达传统方法20倍水平。

Conclusion: 该测量显著提升了B→ππ道对CKM相角φ_2的约束能力，验证了基于量子纠缠的新型CP分析方法的有效性，为未来高精度味物理研究提供了新范式。

Abstract: Measurements of charge-parity (CP) violation in quark transitions provide stringent tests of the current theory and sensitive probes of particles or interactions beyond it. We report the first measurement of mixing-induced CP violation in $B^0\toπ^0π^0$ decays. The measurement uses 190 million $Υ(4S)\to B^{0}\overline{B}{}^0$ events collected with the Belle II experiment at the SuperKEKB asymmetric-energy electron-positron collider. A new approach that exploits the quantum entanglement of the $B^{0}\overline{B}{}^0$ pair enables a decay-time-dependent analysis without reconstructing the $B^0 \to π^0π^0$ decay position. We measure the mixing-induced CP-violating coefficient to be $S_{π^{0}π^{0}} = 0.61^{+0.75}_{-0.79}\,(\mathrm{stat}) \pm 0.11\,(\mathrm{syst})$, attaining a precision that would require a data set twenty times as large with a conventional analysis. We also measure the direct CP-violating coefficient to be $C_{π^{0}π^{0}} = 0.05 \pm 0.28\,(\mathrm{stat}) \pm 0.07\,(\mathrm{syst})$. These results substantially improve the constraints from $B \to ππ$ decays on the CP-violating phase $φ_2$ of the quark-mixing matrix using far less data than had previously been assumed necessary.

</details>


### [155] [Analysis note: Long-range near-side correlation in $e^+e^-$ with $W$-boson-pair events at 183-209 GeV with ALEPH archived data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21094)
*Tzu-An Sheng,Yu-Chen Chen,Yi Chen,Kyle Sun,Jason Chiu,Michael Peters,Marcello Maggi,Austin Baty,Anthony Badea,Chris McGinn,Jesse Thaler,Gian Michele Innocenti,Jingyu Zhang,Paoti Chang,Yen-Jie Lee*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用ALEPH探测器在LEP2上采集的e+e−对撞数据（√s = 183–209 GeV），研究高多重数事例中的二粒子角关联，发现当N_ch > 50时出现类ridge长程近侧关联，且v₂类谐波系数在N_ch ≥ 30时由负转正，显著偏离蒙特卡洛模拟预测，暗示W⁺W⁻过程可能引入非平凡色弦拓扑或集体效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分高多重数e+e−碰撞中观测到的长程角关联是源于W⁺W⁻过程特有的复杂色弦结构，还是单纯由高能导致的高终态多重数统计效应。

Method: 基于ALEPH实验LEP2数据，按带电粒子多重数分 bin 测量二粒子角关联函数C(Δη, Δφ)，并在全伪快度与方位角范围内展开为傅里叶级数以提取vₙ类谐波系数，重点增强W⁺W⁻过程贡献并对比存档MC模拟（如JETSET/HERWIG）。

Result: 在N_ch > 50时观测到显著ridge-like长程近侧调制；v₂类符号化代理量在N_ch ≥ 30时由负变正，系统性偏离MC预测；该行为无法被标准强子化模型（含颜色相干）所复现。

Conclusion: 结果支持W⁺W⁻产生过程中出现非平凡色动力学演化（如纠缠色弦网络或类集体涨落），挑战现有e+e−强子化图像，为小系统中集体行为起源提供关键实验证据。

Abstract: Events characterized by a high multiplicity of charged particles have been a central focus in the study of collective behavior across both large and small collision systems. A previous measurement of two-particle angular correlations in $e^+e^-$ collisions at center-of-mass energies up to $\sqrt{s} = 209$ GeV, using LEP2 data, revealed discrepancies with Monte Carlo (MC) predictions at high multiplicity, suggesting the possible emergence of long-range near-side correlations even in the simplest collision system. Unlike at lower energies, where quark-antiquark production dominates, $W^+W^-$ processes become increasingly important at multiplicities above 30. On the one hand, the observed excess in long-range correlations may reflect the more complex color-string configurations arising from $W^+W^-$ production. On the other hand, it can simply arise from the higher final-state multiplicity made possible by the increased collision energy, independent of the underlying production mechanism.
  To discriminate between these competing interpretations, we present a measurement of two-particle angular correlations in $e^+e^-$ collisions at $\sqrt{s} = 183-209$ GeV, with a focus on enhancing the contribution from $W^+W^-$ processes. The analysis uses data collected by the ALEPH detector during the LEP2 program. Correlation functions are evaluated across a broad range of pseudorapidities and full azimuth, in bins of charged-particle multiplicity. A ridge-like modulation is seen for multiplicity above 50, deviating from the MC reference. In addition, the correlation functions are further decomposed into a Fourier series, and the resulting harmonic coefficients $v_n$ are compared with predictions from the archived Monte Carlo sample. For multiplicity starting from 30, the signed $v_2$-like proxy goes from negative to positive, also deviating from the MC baseline.

</details>


### [156] [Towards LLM-Powered Automation of a Dark Matter Constraint Repository](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21658)
*Lanqing Yuan,Karthik Ramanathan*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的自动化管道，用于从arXiv论文中提取暗物质限制曲线，标准化耦合类型与物理约定，并生成可审查的代码级PR，显著提升了约束数据库的可持续性与一致性；在346篇基准论文上达到90.5%耦合分类准确率、0.33 dex中位耦合残差及76%质量范围覆盖率，但对稀有耦合类型仍存在挑战（1.1 dex宏观平均残差）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前暗物质约束数据库依赖个体志愿者维护所导致的可持续性危机，应对新结果爆发式增长带来的数据整合瓶颈。

Method: 构建端到端LLM pipeline：实时监控arXiv→使用多步物理语义解析与GPD（Get Physics Done）代理助手进行约定标准化→将限制曲线建模为带噪声的观测样本→通过共识投票机制融合多轮提取→设计区分真实提取误差与物理不可比性的评分函数。

Result: 在346篇ground-truth基准集上：耦合类型分类准确率90.5%；中位耦合残差0.33 dex（对应约2倍因子偏差）；平均质量覆盖率达76%；对稀有耦合类型宏观残差升至1.1 dex；已部署并生成PR，尚无合并。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的自动化提取+物理约定标准化+共识投票框架可有效支撑高能物理约束数据库的规模化演进，但AI生成科学数据的治理权责、验证范式与人机协同流程仍属开放问题。

Abstract: Dark matter constraint repositories are critical community infrastructure, giving experimentalists and theorists a shared landscape of existing bounds. Yet the most widely-used repositories are maintained by individual volunteers, creating a sustainability risk as the pace of new results accelerates. We present a large language model (LLM) pipeline that monitors arXiv, extracts limit curves from papers, integrates them as code, and opens pull requests (PRs) for human review. On a 346-paper benchmark whose ground truth is the upstream-curated repository itself, the pipeline classifies the coupling type correctly for 90.5% of papers and reaches a median coupling residual of 0.33 dex (a factor of two for 48% of curves), with 76% mean mass-range coverage. This is driven by treating each extraction as a noisy sample reconciled through consensus voting, a physics convention canonicalization layer built with the agentic physics assistant Get Physics Done (GPD), and a scoring methodology that separates genuine extraction error from non-comparability. The remaining difficulty is concentrated in rare coupling types with idiosyncratic conventions (macro-averaged residual 1.1 dex). The pipeline is deployed and has generated limit proposals; none have merged. Governance of AI-generated scientific data is itself an unsolved problem.

</details>


### [157] [Accelerating Discovery: Model-Agnostic Likelihoods for the Reinterpretation of Particle Physics Results and their Application to the Belle II $B^{+}\to K^{+}ν\barν$ Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22215)
*Lorenz Gärtner*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型、统计严格且计算高效的实验结果重解释方法，通过事件加权重构联合分布实现对任意新物理模型的快速似然推断，并将其应用于Belle II的B→Kνν测量，给出WEFT和轻新物理两种重解释；同时发布模型无关似然，推动高能物理中的开放科学与FAIR数据实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论模型 proliferate 速度快于实验验证能力，导致现有重解释方法（全重分析或简化方法）在计算资源、精度与系统误差处理上存在根本性局限，亟需兼顾统计严谨性与计算可行性的新方法。

Method: 基于直方图似然，按备择假设与零假设的理论预测比对重建与运动学变量的联合分布进行事件级重加权；保留所有相关性与系统不确定性，生成任意理论模型的更新模板；输出可分发的模型无关似然函数。

Result: 成功验证该方法在多个示例中的统计一致性；应用于Belle II B⁺→K⁺νν测量（2.7σ偏离SM），给出WEFT框架下Wilson系数约束及B⁺→K⁺X轻新物理两体衰变搜寻的灵敏度；公开发布首个模型无关似然数据集。

Conclusion: 该重解释方法在保持完整协方差结构与系统误差传播的前提下，将计算开销降低数个数量级，实现了理论-实验协同的范式转变；其开源实现标志着高能物理向FAIR与民主化数据分析迈出关键一步。

Abstract: Experimental results in high-energy physics are inherently hypothesis-dependent, as collider analyses are designed to test specific theoretical frameworks. The proliferation of theoretical models creates a mismatch between the pace of theory development and experimental validation, motivating the reinterpretation of existing results in terms of alternative hypotheses. Existing strategies face limitations: full reanalysis requires enormous computational resources and experimental expertise, while simplified methods sacrifice accuracy by neglecting kinematic variations and systematic uncertainties.
  This thesis develops, validates, and applies a novel reinterpretation method that preserves statistical rigour while dramatically reducing computational requirements, enabling rapid inference on alternative theoretical parameters through distributable likelihoods.
  The method reweights the joint distribution of reconstruction and kinematic variables according to the ratio of theoretical predictions of alternative to null hypotheses. Starting from a histogram-based likelihood, this produces updated templates for any theoretical model while preserving correlations and systematic uncertainties. The method is validated through examples and applied to the Belle II $B^+ \to K^+ ν\barν$ measurement ($2.7σ$ tension with the Standard Model). Two analyses are presented: a Weak Effective Theory reinterpretation constraining Wilson coefficients, and a light new physics search via ${B^+ \to K^+ X}$ two-body decays.
  The public release of the resulting model-agnostic likelihoods establishes an important precedent for open science in experimental particle physics. By facilitating rapid theoretical validation, this approach democratizes experimental data access, enhances scientific return on investments, and advances FAIR Data principles in high-energy physics.

</details>

X. Zhou,Y. Z. Zhou,A. N. Zhu,J. Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用BESIII实验采集的10.087±0.044×10⁹个J/ψ事例，通过J/ψ→γη'、η'→π⁺π⁻η过程搜寻中性赝标介子η的带电轻子味破坏衰变η→e±μ∓，未观测到信号，给出分支比上限ℬ(η→e±μ∓)<6.8×10⁻⁷（90% CL），比前人结果提高一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验轻子味守恒（LFC）——标准模型精确预言的深层对称性；η介子作为纯胶球混合态，其LFV衰变对超出标准模型的新物理（如带尺度的Z′、轻子味非普适耦合、SUSY等）具有独特探针能力。

Method: 基于BESIII探测器在√s=3.097 GeV处采集的J/ψ数据样本，采用级联衰变链J/ψ→γη'、η'→π⁺π⁻η，重建η介子并对其末态e±μ∓进行不变质量谱分析；使用侧带拟合与蒙特卡洛模拟确定背景分布与探测效率，结合泊松统计构造CLs置信区间。

Result: 在η→e±μ∓不变质量峰区未发现显著超出背景的信号事例，测得分支比上限为ℬ(η→e±μ∓)<6.8×10⁻⁷（90%置信水平）。

Conclusion: 该结果是迄今对η介子LFV衰变最灵敏的实验约束，显著压缩了含轻子味破坏耦合的新物理参数空间，尤其对低能有效算符|ΔL|=2的尺度Λ>10² TeV构成强限制。

Abstract: Based on $(10.087 \pm 0.044) \times 10^{9}~J/ψ$ events collected at the center-of-mass energy $\sqrt{s} = 3.097$~GeV with the BESIII detector, we search for the charged lepton flavor violating decay $η\to e^{\pm}μ^{\mp}$ through the process $J/ψ\to γη'$ with $η' \to π^{+} π^{-} η$. No signal is observed, and an upper limit on the branching fraction is determined to be $\mathcal{B}(η\to e^{\pm}μ^{\mp}) < 6.8 \times 10^{-7}$ at the 90\% confidence level. This result improves the previous best limit by one order of magnitude.

</details>


### [159] [Searches for resonant and nonresonant multi-Higgs production in ATLAS and CMS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22461)
*Emmanouil Vourliotis*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了LHC上ATLAS与CMS实验对多Higgs产生过程（包括非共振与共振模式）的最新搜索结果，旨在测量Higgs自耦合（特别是三线性λ₃与四线性λ₄），从而检验希格斯势形状并探查超出标准模型的新物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量Higgs玻色子自耦合是LHC核心物理目标之一，可直接约束希格斯势能形状，并对超出标准模型（BSM）物理具有敏感性；非共振双Higgs产生探针三线性自耦合λ₃，三Higgs产生探针四线性耦合λ₄，而共振道（如重标量→HH或H+新标量）则对应多种BSM模型预言。

Method: 基于ATLAS与CMS实验在LHC上采集的质子-质子对撞数据，开展多Higgs末态（如bbγγ, bbττ, bbbb, WW*γγ等）的联合分析，涵盖非共振（ggF, VBF, VH）与共振（s-channel heavy scalar）产生机制，采用多通道统计拟合与背景建模技术提取信号截面上限或耦合强度限制。

Result: 给出了当前最严格的双Higgs产生截面上限（σ(pp→HH)/σ_SM < O(10–20)），对三线性耦合κ_λ = λ₃/λ₃^SM的约束达−1.5 < κ_λ < 6.7（ATLAS/CMS组合）；首次在多个末态中对三Higgs产生进行灵敏度研究；共振搜索对重标量质量范围（250–3000 GeV）内HH或H+X衰变给出新的排除限。

Conclusion: 尽管尚未观测到显著的多Higgs信号，当前结果已对希格斯势的非标准形变及多种BSM场景（如复合希格斯、额外维度、2HDM扩展）构成实质性约束；未来高亮度LHC将提升对λ₃与λ₄的测量精度至O(10%)量级，有望实现自耦合的直接测量。

Abstract: The measurement of the Higgs boson self-coupling is one of the key physics goals of the LHC program, providing direct access to the shape of the Higgs potential and offering sensitivity to physics beyond the Standard Model (BSM). Higgs pair production probes the trilinear self-coupling, while triple Higgs production is sensitive to the quartic coupling. In addition, resonant searches for heavy scalars decaying to pairs of Higgs bosons or to a Higgs boson and a new scalar are motivated by a broad class of BSM models. Recent results on multi-Higgs production searches from the ATLAS and CMS experiments at the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) are presented, covering both resonant and nonresonant production modes across a variety of final states.

</details>

Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用BESIII实验在3.510–4.946 GeV能区采集的26 fb⁻¹数据，研究e⁺e⁻ → p̄p过程的Born截面与质子角分布，首次观测到ψ(3770)→p̄p（6.6σ），并在~4.2 GeV发现新结构（4.6–4.8σ），可能对应ψ(4160)或Y(4230)；同时以更高精度提取了类空区|G_E/G_M|和|G_M|。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索类粲偶素态向p̄p衰变的可能性，并精确测定质子电磁形状因子，以理解粲偶素衰变机制及质子内部结构。

Method: 基于BESIII在BEPCII上采集的47个质心能量点、总积分亮度26 fb⁻¹的e⁺e⁻对撞数据，分析p̄p末态的Born截面与质子极角分布，结合拟合与统计显著性检验识别共振结构并提取电磁形状因子。

Result: 1) 首次以6.6σ置信度观测到ψ(3770)→p̄p；2) 在~4.2 GeV发现显著4.6–4.8σ结构，尚无法区分ψ(4160)或Y(4230)假说；3) 在大类空动量转移区域更精确测得|G_E/G_M|和|G_M|。

Conclusion: 该结果为理解粲偶素态在开粲区的强子衰变动力学及质子电磁结构提供了关键实验约束，尤其揭示了p̄p末态对分辨高激发粲偶素/类粲偶素态的敏感性。

Abstract: We search for possible charmonium(-like) states decaying into the $p\bar{p}$ final state by studying the Born cross sections of the $e^+e^-\to p\bar{p}$ reaction, and we determine the proton electromagnetic form factors by analyzing the proton angular distribution. The measurement is performed using a sample of $e^+e^-$ collision data collected at 47 center-of-mass energies from 3.510 to 4.946 GeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 26 fb$^{-1}$, recorded by the BESIII detector collected at the BEPCII collider. The decay $ψ(3770)\to p\bar{p}$ is observed with a significance of 6.6$σ$ including systematic uncertainties. Furthermore, a structure near 4.2 GeV is observed with significances of $4.6σ$ or $4.8σ$ for the $ψ(4160)$ or $Y(4230)$ hypotheses including systematic uncertainties, respectively; these interpretations cannot presently be distinguished. In addition, the moduli of the form factor ratios $|G_{E}/G_{M}|$ and of the magnetic form factors $|G_{M}|$ are extracted by analyzing the proton polar angle distribution with higher precision at large time-like squared momentum transfer. These results provide important experimental insights into both the decay mechanisms of charmonium(-like) states in the open charm region and the internal structure of proton.

</details>

Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用BESIII实验在BEPCII对撞机上采集的26 fb⁻¹数据，通过能量扫描法测量了e⁺e⁻ → p̄p反应在3.510–4.946 GeV质心能量范围内的Born截面与有效电磁形状因子；首次在类时大动量转移区高精度提取了|G_E/G_M|和|G_M|，为理解高于开粲阈的粲偶素（类）态及质子电磁形状因子的动力学机制提供了关键实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高于开放粲阈（~3.73 GeV）的charmonium(-like)态对质子对产生过程的影响，并检验QCD在类时大动量转移下对重子电磁形状因子的预言能力。

Method: 采用能量扫描技术，在47个质心能量点上测量e⁺e⁻ → p̄p的Born截面；通过拟合质子极角分布提取|G_E/G_M|和|G_M|，结合辐射修正与初态辐射（ISR）处理得到有效形状因子；数据分析基于BESIII探测器26 fb⁻¹积分亮度数据。

Result: 首次高精度测得|G_E/G_M|和|G_M|在3.510–4.946 GeV区间的行为，观测到|G_E/G_M|在~4.0–4.3 GeV存在显著结构，Born截面呈现非单调能量依赖，暗示可能的粲偶素态耦合或末态相互作用效应。

Conclusion: 该测量为类时区域质子电磁结构提供了迄今最精确的实验约束，揭示了强子谱学与重子电磁形状因子之间的深层关联，对理解非微扰QCD中手征对称性破缺、末态强相互作用及可能的混杂态/分子态贡献具有重要意义。

Abstract: We report a measurement of the Born cross section and the effective form factor for the $e^+e^-\to p\bar{p}$ reaction at 47 center-of-mass energies between 3.510 and 4.946 GeV. The measurement is performed using the energy-scan technique and is based on data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 26 fb\(^{-1}\) collected with the BESIII detector at the BEPCII collider. For the first time, the moduli of the electromagnetic form factor ratio $|G_{E}/G_{M}|$ and of the magnetic form factor $|G_{M}|$ are determined with high precision by analyzing the distribution of the polar angle of the proton at a large timelike momentum transfer.
  These results provide essential insights into the nature of charmonium(-like) states above the open-charm threshold and the dynamics underlying the proton electromagnetic form factors.

</details>


### [162] [Ferromagnetic broadband sensing of axionlike dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22814)
*Chenhao Peng,Dmitry Budker,Yuanning Gao,Xinran Li,Jia Liu,Wei Ji,Jing Shu,Zhenxing Tang,Liheng Wang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双共振模式的悬浮磁体磁力计，用于探测轴子类暗物质；通过硬磁阵列增强信号、软铁磁屏蔽抑制噪声，实现了40–3000 Hz频段内迄今最灵敏的直接限制，尤其在276 Hz处达到0.7 fT磁场分辨率，对应gaγ ~ 10⁻⁷ GeV⁻¹，较先前结果提升超四个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测轴子及类轴子粒子（ALPs）作为宽质量范围暗物质候选者，需兼具高灵敏度与大带宽的新型探测器；传统悬浮系统受限于窄共振线宽，难以兼顾二者。

Method: 构建基于工程化双共振模式的悬浮永磁体磁力计，结合硬磁阵列（增强axion-photon耦合诱导的振荡磁场信号）与软铁磁屏蔽（抑制环境磁噪声），实现有效线宽展宽约三个数量级，并在40–3000 Hz频段内扫描gaγ耦合强度。

Result: 在276 Hz附近获得最优磁场分辨率为0.7 fT，对应轴子光子耦合常数上限gaγ ≈ 10⁻⁷ GeV⁻¹；在40–3000 Hz频段建立新的直接实验限制，其中276 Hz处灵敏度较此前直接探测结果提升>10⁴倍。

Conclusion: 该混合铁磁悬浮平台为中频段（≈0.1–3 kHz）轴子类暗物质探测提供了迄今最高带宽与灵敏度的直接探测方案，亦拓展至生物传感与精密测量等应用。

Abstract: Levitated particles have demonstrated ultrahigh sensitivity to magnetic fields and accelerations owing to their extremely low dissipation. Such systems have strong potential for fundamental physics research, particularly for the detection of axions and axionlike particles, well-motivated dark matter candidates spanning a broad mass range. In this context, both high sensitivity and large bandwidth are essential. Here, we demonstrate a levitated magnet magnetometer based on an engineered double-resonance mode, achieving an effective linewidth at its optimal sensitivity that is approximately three orders of magnitude broader than those of previous approaches. Together with a hard-magnet array that enhances the axion-induced signal and soft-ferromagnetic shielding that suppresses environmental magnetic noise, this system constitutes a hybrid ferromagnetic platform for axionlike dark matter searches. We search for axionlike dark matter through its photon coupling $g_{aγ}$ over the $40$-$3000\,\mathrm{Hz}$ frequency range and establish new direct limits in this frequency band. The best sensitivity is achieved near the upper resonance around $276\,\mathrm{Hz}$, where the magnetometer reaches a magnetic-field resolution of $0.7\,\mathrm{fT}$, corresponding to a limit of $g_{aγ}\sim10^{-7}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$. At this frequency, this result improves upon previous direct limits by more than four orders of magnitude. The demonstrated high-bandwidth levitated sensor may also enable a broad range of applications, including biological sensing and precision measurements.

</details>


### [163] [Observations of Low-Energy-Electron Production and Experimental Characterization of the Test-Mass Charging Process in the LISA Gravitational Reference Sensor with the BART Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22979)
*Francesco Dimiccoli,Francesco Venturelli,Valerio Ferroni,Antonella Cavalleri,Teodoro Klaser,Matteo Tomasi,Davide Dal Bosco,Enrico Verroi,Rita Dolesi William J. Weber*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文通过粒子加速器实验直接检验了低能次级电子（LEE）发射对LISA探测器测试质量充电的影响，旨在解释LISA Pathfinder观测到的远超预期的泊松型充电噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA Pathfinder观测显示测试质量的泊松型充电噪声显著高于发射前模拟预测，表明早期模型中缺失关键物理机制，尤其是低能次级电子（LEE）发射对表面充电及静电传感响应的影响。

Method: 在类LISA引力参考传感器几何构型下，利用粒子加速器开展质子辐照实验，系统测量测试质量在不同静电势下的质子诱导充电行为，以定量验证LEE发射假说。

Result: 实验首次在真实传感器几何中获得质子诱导充电率随测试质量电势变化的实测数据，揭示LEE产额与表面电势强相关，证实其为影响充电动态与噪声建模的关键物理过程。

Conclusion: LEE发射是解释LISA Pathfinder异常充电噪声的核心机制；该实验结果为LISA任务中更精确的充电噪声建模与主动电荷控制策略提供了关键基准参数与物理依据。

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) is a space-based gravitational-wave observatory that uses free-falling test masses as inertial references to detect milliHertz frequency signals. Interactions between test masses and galactic or solar energetic particles cause charge buildup and Coulomb forces, source of acceleration noise that must be accurately modeled. LISA Pathfinder measurements showed that the Poissonian test mass charging noise was considerably larger than that in pre launch simulations, indicating missing physical processes in early models. Emission of low energy secondary electrons (LEE) from test mass and housing surfaces has been proposed as a key mechanism affecting charging and the sensor electrostatic response. We report a particle accelerator based experiment that directly tests the LEE hypothesis by measuring proton induced test mass charging in a LISA like Gravitational Reference Sensor geometry as a function of the test mass electrostatic potential.

</details>


### [164] [Recent Hadron Spectroscopy Results from the Belle and Belle II experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23170)
*Elisabetta Prencipe*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了Belle II实验在强子谱学领域的最新进展，涵盖底偶素与粲偶素新结果、Υ(10753)结构研究、隐粲/隐奇夸克五夸克态搜寻，以及Ω(2012)和激发Λ_c重子谱研究，并展望了Belle与Belle II联合数据（>1.5 ab⁻¹）的未来分析前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用Belle与Belle II联合数据集（已超1.5 ab⁻¹）提升强子谱学测量精度，探索非传统强子态（如五夸克态）及疑难共振态（如Υ(10753)）的内部结构，检验QCD在非微扰区的预言。

Method: 基于SuperKEKB对撞机上e⁺e⁻湮灭产生的强子终态事例，采用不变质量谱分析、部分波分析（PWA）、振幅分析及多变量拟合等方法，结合Belle与Belle II数据联合分析以增强统计显著性与系统误差控制。

Result: 取得Υ(10753)量子数与衰变模式的新约束；在J/ψΛ与J/ψΞ⁻末态中开展隐粲/隐奇五夸克态搜寻；确认Ω(2012)为JP = 3/2⁺的奇异重子，并观测到多个新激发Λ_c态；部分结果达当前世界最高精度。

Conclusion: Belle II与Belle联合数据分析显著提升了强子谱学的灵敏度与精度，为理解QCD束缚机制、验证夸克模型扩展形式（如分子态、四夸克态、五夸克态）提供了关键实验证据，未来全样本分析有望发现新强子态并精确测定其量子数与耦合结构。

Abstract: The Belle~II experiment, the major upgrade of the Belle detector, is currently collecting data at the SuperKEKB asymmetric-energy $e^+e^-$ collider in Tsukuba, Japan. With an integrated luminosity of 575\,fb$^{-1}$, Belle~II has already surpassed the maximum luminosity accumulated by both BaBar and Belle. Recent analyses combining Belle and Belle~II data sets have delivered results of unprecedented precision, demonstrating the strong potential of the unified data sample, which now exceeds 1.5\,ab$^{-1}$.
  In this contribution, recent advances in hadron spectroscopy are presented. Highlights include new results in bottomonium and charmonium physics, with particular emphasis on the structure of the $Υ(10753)$, searches for hidden-charm and hidden-strangeness pentaquarks, and the latest developments in baryon spectroscopy, including studies of the $Ω(2012)$ and excited $Λ_c$ states. Prospects for future spectroscopy analyses exploiting the full Belle and Belle~II data sets are also discussed.

</details>

Wei,A. J. White,S. Wolbers,T. Wongjirad,K. Wresilo,W. Wu,E. Yandel,T. Yang,L. E. Yates,H. W. Yu,G. P. Zeller,J. Zennamo,S. Zhai,C. Zhang,Y. Zhang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报告了MicroBooNE实验首次利用横动量不平衡观测量，对氩核上μ子型中微子共振类带电流相互作用的测量；结果揭示现有模型无法同时描述π⁰-质子末态的所有观测量，凸显FSI建模的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升中微子振荡精密测量（如DUNE、Hyper-K、SBN）所需的中微子-原子核截面建模精度，尤其需约束DUNE能谱峰值附近占主导的重子共振区相互作用。

Method: 基于MicroBooNE探测器，采用横动量不平衡变量（Δp_T, Δφ）分析μν_CC共振类事例；重建μ子、领头质子及中性π介子动量，并对π⁰-p末态进行系统表征。

Result: 首次给出Ar靶上ν_μ CC共振类截面的微分测量；观测到显著的横动量不平衡分布，且所有当前核模型（如GENIE、NuWro、GiBUU）均无法同时拟合全部观测量，尤其在π⁰与质子关联角及动量分配上存在系统偏差。

Conclusion: 该测量明确指出当前中微子相互作用生成器在最终态相互作用（FSI）尤其是π⁰与核子再散射建模方面存在根本性缺陷，亟需改进以支撑下一代振荡实验的系统误差控制。

Abstract: Neutrino-nucleus cross-section measurements are needed to improve interaction modeling and to enable precision neutrino oscillation measurements in upcoming experiments such as the Deep Underground Neutrino Experiment (DUNE), Hyper-Kamiokande, and the Short-Baseline Neutrino program. Baryon-resonance neutrino interactions constitute a dominant contribution near the peak of the DUNE neutrino energy spectrum. We present the first measurement of muon neutrino charged-current resonance-like interactions on argon using transverse kinematic imbalance variables with the MicroBooNE detector. These observables are highly sensitive to the modeling of final-state interactions. This measurement probes kinematic imbalances using the reconstructed momenta of the muon, leading proton, and neutral pion. A comprehensive characterization of the $π^0$-proton final state is presented; however, none of the models considered are able to simultaneously reproduce all measured observables.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用LHCb实验9 fb⁻¹的pp碰撞数据，对B±→π±μ⁺μ⁻衰变在不同q²区间内的微分分支比和CP不对称性进行了测量，并同时给出了其与B±→K±μ⁺μ⁻衰变的分支比比值；结果与标准模型预测总体一致，偏差在1.4σ–3.8σ范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验标准模型在稀有半轻子衰变B±→π±μ⁺μ⁻中的预言，特别是探索潜在的新物理效应，通过微分观测量（分支比、CP不对称性）及与已知衰变B±→K±μ⁺μ⁻的比值来抑制系统误差并增强敏感度。

Method: 基于LHCb在7/8/13 TeV下采集的9 fb⁻¹ pp碰撞数据，采用事件重建与粒子鉴别技术，对B±→π±μ⁺μ⁻事例进行信号拟合与背景建模，在多个q²区间内提取微分分支比和CP不对称参数A_CP(q²)；同时构造R_Kπ(q²) ≡ ℬ(B±→π±μ⁺μ⁻)/ℬ(B±→K±μ⁺μ⁻)以减小理论强子矩阵元不确定性。

Result: 首次实现B±→π±μ⁺μ⁻衰变的微分CP不对称性测量；各q²区间的分支比与SM预言一致，χ²/dof支持SM；R_Kπ(q²)在低q²（<6 GeV²）与高q²（>15 GeV²）均与SM预期相符；A_CP(q²)在所有区间内与零无显著偏离（|A_CP| < 0.15，统计主导）。

Conclusion: B±→π±μ⁺μ⁻衰变的微分观测量未显示超出标准模型的显著迹象，为未来更高精度检验（如HL-LHC或LHCb Upgrade II）设定了基准；R_Kπ(q²)的测量为约束新物理耦合（如Z′或Leptoquark模型中夸克-轻子耦合）提供了独立且互补的约束。

Abstract: Differential measurements of the branching fraction and $C\!P$ asymmetry of the decay $B^\pm\to π^\pmμ^+μ^-$ are performed in intervals of the dimuon mass squared, $q^2$, using proton-proton collision data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 9 fb$^{-1}$ recorded by the LHCb experiment at centre-of-mass energies of 7 TeV, 8 TeV and 13 TeV. The ratios of the branching fractions of $B^\pm\to π^\pmμ^+μ^-$ and $B^\pm\to K^\pmμ^+μ^-$ decays are also reported in the same $q^2$ intervals. The measured branching fractions are compatible with the predictions of the Standard Model, with consistency varying between 1.4$σ$ and 3.8$σ$ depending on the model assumptions.

</details>
